Title: De Trinitatis erroribus libri septem
Creator: Michael Servetus (Miguel Servet)
Publisher: [Haguenau]: [Johann Setzer], 1531 (imprint as given in the host record; the printer is not named on the title page in this OCR)
Publication Date: 1531
Rights: Public Domain (published 1531; public domain by date; the host record carries no rights field)
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item McGillLibrary-rbsc_004132305-18081, file rbsc_004132305_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, one-world agent for anti-trinitarian-currents-servetus-polish-brethr), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: none on the item (contributor McGill University Library, Rare Books and Special Collections).
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from long-s to s normalisation (0 occurrences). Rights basis restated: a 1531 imprint is public domain by date; the host item carries no rights field and is not access-restricted.
Content note: Servetus's first book, seven books on the errors of the Trinity (1531), in Latin, the work for which he was first condemned. The OCR is badly garbled in places (the 10 and 50 percent samples are readable Latin with errors; the 30 and 90 percent samples fall in noise, and the word-like share of tokens is about 0.79). Second witness for exact wording until a cleaner scan is found (Mark's OCR ruling, 2026-09-25). No public-domain English exists: Wilbur's 1932 translation (Harvard Theological Studies 16) was renewed in 1960 (R263240) and its archive.org copy is lending-only.
